          (hash "fin"

              (hash "chts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr sr))
            (cat
              (cat

                    (enc s spk (privk ca)))
                  (enc
                    (hash "scv" (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y))

                    (hash "shts" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))) cr

                    (hash "scv" (cat cr (exp (gen) x) sr (exp (gen) y))

    ((recv
       (hash "sats" cr sr
         (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))))

          (hash "ms" (hash "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))))

    ((recv (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y))))
      (send (cat "hs" (exp (gen) (mul x y)))))
    ((recv (cat (exp (gen) y) x)) (send (cat (exp (gen) y) x))))
